subsidiaries, the “Group”) is a leading dairy farming operator      團」），為中華人民共和國（         「中國」  ）乳牛畜牧營運商
and raw milk producer in the industry in the People’s Republic      及原料奶生產商的行業翹楚。我們在國內投資及運
of China (the “PRC”). We invest in and operate 42 farm              營42個牧場公司，覆蓋13個省份和自治區，於本期
companies in Chinese Mainland, spanning 13 provinces and            末飼養約44.5萬頭乳牛，年化產奶量達259萬噸。
autonomous regions, with approximately 445,000 dairy cows           本公司首創「種養加一體化、零距離2小時」的生產模
being raised by the end of the current period, and annualized       式，是中國首家一體化生產模式並通過SGS認證的
milk yield reaching 2.59 million tons. The Company pioneered        企業。現代牧業品牌奶連續十一年在素有食品界「諾
the production model of “Zero-distance Integration of Forage        貝爾獎」之稱的世界食品品質評鑒大會中獲得金獎。
Grass Planting, Cows Breeding and Milk Processing within Two        近年，本集團的業務板塊已擴至數智平台、飼料、
Hours”, being the first in the PRC with integrated production       牧草及育種等新業務，並持續為集團的發展帶來貢

INDUSTRY OVERVIEW                                                               行業概覽

In the first half of 2024, China’s dairy industry faced significant pressure,   2024年上半年，我國奶業發展面臨較大壓力，乳品
characterized by weak consumer demand, oversupply of raw milk,                  消費市場疲弱，原料奶供應過剩，牛肉價格持續下
declining beef prices, and decreased profitability for dairy farming and        跌，奶牛養殖和乳品加工企業效益下滑。據國家統
processing enterprises. According to the National Bureau of Statistics,         計局統計，上半年我國原料奶產量同比增加3.4%。
China’s raw milk production increased by 3.4% year-on-year in the first         然而，原料奶價格卻呈現持續下滑的趨勢，對奶牛
half of 2024. However, raw milk prices continued to decline, putting            養殖的利潤率造成壓力。為了應對這些困難，部分
pressure on the profitability of dairy farming. To address these challenges,    乳業大省推出了奶業紓困政策，支持乳製品加工企
several major dairy-producing provinces introduced relief policies to           業在銷售淡季及時收購生鮮乳，減輕庫存壓力，保
support dairy processing enterprises by purchasing fresh milk during the        護奶農利益。政府補貼資金的到位，發揮了關鍵作
off-season, which reduced inventory pressure and protected the interests        用，極大地保障了企業的收奶積極性，對奶源的穩
of dairy farmers. The timely allocation of government subsidies played          定性起到了至關重要的作用。
a crucial role in ensuring the active participation of enterprises in milk
collection and stabilizing the milk supply.

In the international market, despite China being a major importer of            在國際市場上，即便中國是新西蘭乳製品進口大
New Zealand dairy products, the zero-tariff policy on milk powder               戶，但2024年1月1日起實施的奶粉零關稅對中國奶
being implemented on 1 January 2024 has had a limited impact on the             業影響有限。加之國際原奶主產國均在控制產量，
Chinese dairy industry. Additionally, with major global raw milk producers      在 2023年 8月 全 脂 大 包 粉 價 格 跌 至 2706美 元 ╱ 噸
controlling production, the auction price of whole milk powder has              的低點後，全脂奶粉拍賣價格快速回升。今年5月
rebounded rapidly after hitting a low of US$2,706/ton in August 2023.           起，大包粉折算成液態乳的價格已高於國內生鮮乳
Since May of this year, the price of whole milk powder converted to liquid      價格，6月全脂大包粉折算成液態乳的價格約為3.6
milk has exceeded that of domestic raw milk. In June, the price of whole        元╱公斤，而國內生鮮乳主產區的市場價為3.3元╱
milk powder converted to liquid milk was approximately RMB3.6/kg,               公斤。2024年上半年我國乳製品的進口量和進口額
while the market price in major domestic raw milk production areas was          都出現了下降。我國共進口各類乳製品130.8萬噸，
RMB3.3/kg. Both the import volume and value of dairy products in China          同比減少15.6%；進口額為54.35億美元，同比減少
declined in the first half of 2024. China imported a total of 1.308 million     22.4%，這也是自2015年以來中國乳製品進口量連
tons of various dairy products, a year-on-year decrease of 15.6%, and           續第三年下降。其中，大包粉的進口量為38.27萬
the import value was 5.435 billion US dollars, a year-on-year decrease of       噸，同比減少20.7%；進口額為13.45億美元，同比
22.4%, marking the third consecutive year of decline since 2015. Among          減少27.6%；平均價格為3,515美元╱噸，同比下降
them, the import volume of milk powder was 382,700 tons, a year-                8.6%。這些數據反映了國內原奶階段性供需失衡的
on-year decrease of 20.7%, and the import value was 1.345 billion US            局面，也導致乳企降低了對外部乳製品的依賴。
dollars, a year-on-year decrease of 27.6%, with an average price of 3,515
US dollars per ton, a year-on-year decrease of 8.6%. These figures reflect
the temporary imbalance between domestic supply and demand of raw
milk, leading dairy enterprises to reduce their reliance on imported dairy

    Thousand Village Renovation’ Project to Effectively Promote Rural             個中央一號文件。提出「完善液態奶標準，規範復原

the Group has adjusted its working capital management strategy in a           2023年 以 來 ， 本 集 團 適 時 調 整 了 營 運 資 金 管 理 策
timely manner to reduce the occupation of working capital by extending        略，通過加大供應鏈金融支付的方式延長了應付款

period. The Group expects to promote supply chain finance payments            運資金佔用及相應減少財務開支。

value chain integration, and employee empowerment. Our management               高、成效效果要顯、時效反應要迅。
will focus on four key areas: strong profitability, high turnover efficiency,
evident results, and rapid response.

Driven by innovation, we will continue to actively contribute to the            以創新驅動高質量發展，牧業將繼續積極承擔國家
revitalization of China’s dairy industry, leading the sector towards digital    奶業振興之責，引領行業邁向數智創新、勵行低碳
innovation and a sustainable development model that emphasizes low-             環保的可持續發展模式，並持續完善全產業鏈生
carbon and environmentally friendly practices. We will continue to              態，打造全球領先且具社會責任的牧業集團。
improve our full industry value ecosystem, striving to become a globally
leading and socially responsible dairy farming group.

adopted by the Company on 9 September 2016 (the “Share Award                       勵計劃（「股份獎勵計劃I」），由於股份獎勵計劃I的供

I has reached its limit in 2017, the Company on 26 March 2018 adopted              3月26日採納另一新股份獎勵計劃（ 「股份獎勵計劃

with the Company in order to achieve sustainable development of the                獎勵計劃II僅由本公司的現有股份撥付。

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE                                                          企業管治

The Company has adopted the code provisions set out in the Corporate          本公司已採納載於上市規則附錄C1所載的企業管治
Governance Code and Corporate Governance Report (“CG Code”) as set            守則及企業管治報告（
                                                                                       「企業管治守則」）的守則條文。
out in Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules. The Company has, throughout          期內，本公司一直遵守載於企業管治守則的守則條
the period, complied with the code provisions set out in the CG Code.         文。

Code provision C.1.6 of the CG Code provides, among other things, that        企 業 管 治 守 則 的 守 則 條 文 第 C.1.6條 規 定（其 中 包
